Surah: 80. Follow the brief below (surah_map.md) exactly. The evidence is text.md (the surah), dictionary.md (every root of the surah's words, each branch with the classical dictionaries' own phrases), channels.md and hft.md (both are earlier readers' proposals: ignore their judgements (grades, strength or confidence labels, reading types, statements of what a reading may or may not do) and make your own) and your own knowledge of Arabic and the Quran. Return only the map as your final message.

===== _commentary/v16/prompts/r2/surah_map.md =====
Read the surah as one text and write its map of image chains: the lexical images that run through several of
its ayat and join them into one scene, process or movement. A later writer will read one ayah at a time, with
only that ayah's own dictionary. The map lets that writer hear what the ayah's words carry in the surah as a
whole, including senses whose evidence sits under the words of other ayat.

Your evidence is the surah text, the dictionary of every root in the surah (each branch with the classical
dictionaries' own phrases), earlier readers' channel review (channels.md) and activation hypotheses (hft.md),
and your own knowledge of Arabic and the Quran. Where a member or passage comes from memory rather than from
the dictionary or the text, say so. Do not use tools, delegate, browse or inspect files.

The channel review and the HFT records are proposals by earlier readers. Ignore their judgements: grades,
strength or confidence labels, reading types, words such as "surprising", "exploratory" or "latent", and every
statement of what a reading may or may not do. Make your own judgement from the surah's words, the dictionary
phrases and the Quran. Do not rediscover what they already assembled: start from their chains, test each one
against the dictionary phrases and the text, and join, extend, split or correct them. Where their wording
abstracts a member, go back to the dictionary phrase and name what it actually says.

A chain belongs on the map when its members are senses the dictionary attests for words that stand in the
surah, and together they make one image or process that the surah's wording or sequence lets a listener hear.
A member need not be the sense that translates its word in its own ayah; a chain is heard across the surah,
not in one word. Say plainly when a member is a rare sense or is attested only inside a fixed expression, and
keep it. When the dictionary itself joins two of the surah's words in one phrase, quote that phrase: it is
the strongest evidence a chain can have. Keep a scene at the level of its objects, their parts and their
operation. When proposals share members, do not fold one into another's more abstract function unless nothing
concrete is lost. Carry every chain that meets this test, however unusual; leave out proposals that do not.

Write the map in English, with Arabic quoted exactly (surah wording from the text, dictionary phrases from the
dictionary). It is working material for the writer, not commentary prose.

1. `## Chains`. For each chain, a `###` heading naming the image. One paragraph on what the image is and how it
   moves through the surah. Then its members, one line each: the ayah ref, the word as it stands in the text,
   the root and branch id, the dictionary's own phrase quoted exactly, and what this member contributes to the
   image. Add Quran passages outside the surah, with exact refs, where they stage or confirm the chain.
2. `## Ayat`. For each ayah in order: which chains it opens, advances or completes, and what its words add to
   each.
3. `## Not carried`. Each channel subchannel and each HFT record you did not carry into a chain, one line each:
   its name and why.

No ranking and no labels of strength or confidence. No list of what a writer must include. There is no length
target and no required number of chains or members.


===== _commentary/v16/work/s080/surah.r2/text.md =====
# Surah 80

- 80:1 عَبَسَ وَتَوَلَّىٰٓ
- 80:2 أَن جَآءَهُ ٱلْأَعْمَىٰ
- 80:3 وَمَا يُدْرِيكَ لَعَلَّهُۥ يَزَّكَّىٰٓ
- 80:4 أَوْ يَذَّكَّرُ فَتَنفَعَهُ ٱلذِّكْرَىٰٓ
- 80:5 أَمَّا مَنِ ٱسْتَغْنَىٰ
- 80:6 فَأَنتَ لَهُۥ تَصَدَّىٰ
- 80:7 وَمَا عَلَيْكَ أَلَّا يَزَّكَّىٰ
- 80:8 وَأَمَّا مَن جَآءَكَ يَسْعَىٰ
- 80:9 وَهُوَ يَخْشَىٰ
- 80:10 فَأَنتَ عَنْهُ تَلَهَّىٰ
- 80:11 كَلَّآ إِنَّهَا تَذْكِرَةٌۭ
- 80:12 فَمَن شَآءَ ذَكَرَهُۥ
- 80:13 فِى صُحُفٍۢ مُّكَرَّمَةٍۢ
- 80:14 مَّرْفُوعَةٍۢ مُّطَهَّرَةٍۭ
- 80:15 بِأَيْدِى سَفَرَةٍۢ
- 80:16 كِرَامٍۭ بَرَرَةٍۢ
- 80:17 قُتِلَ ٱلْإِنسَٰنُ مَآ أَكْفَرَهُۥ
- 80:18 مِنْ أَىِّ شَىْءٍ خَلَقَهُۥ
- 80:19 مِن نُّطْفَةٍ خَلَقَهُۥ فَقَدَّرَهُۥ
- 80:20 ثُمَّ ٱلسَّبِيلَ يَسَّرَهُۥ
- 80:21 ثُمَّ أَمَاتَهُۥ فَأَقْبَرَهُۥ
- 80:22 ثُمَّ إِذَا شَآءَ أَنشَرَهُۥ
- 80:23 كَلَّا لَمَّا يَقْضِ مَآ أَمَرَهُۥ
- 80:24 فَلْيَنظُرِ ٱلْإِنسَٰنُ إِلَىٰ طَعَامِهِۦٓ
- 80:25 أَنَّا صَبَبْنَا ٱلْمَآءَ صَبًّۭا
- 80:26 ثُمَّ شَقَقْنَا ٱلْأَرْضَ شَقًّۭا
- 80:27 فَأَنۢبَتْنَا فِيهَا حَبًّۭا
- 80:28 وَعِنَبًۭا وَقَضْبًۭا
- 80:29 وَزَيْتُونًۭا وَنَخْلًۭا
- 80:30 وَحَدَآئِقَ غُلْبًۭا
- 80:31 وَفَٰكِهَةًۭ وَأَبًّۭا
- 80:32 مَّتَٰعًۭا لَّكُمْ وَلِأَنْعَٰمِكُمْ
- 80:33 فَإِذَا جَآءَتِ ٱلصَّآخَّةُ
- 80:34 يَوْمَ يَفِرُّ ٱلْمَرْءُ مِنْ أَخِيهِ
- 80:35 وَأُمِّهِۦ وَأَبِيهِ
- 80:36 وَصَٰحِبَتِهِۦ وَبَنِيهِ
- 80:37 لِكُلِّ ٱمْرِئٍۢ مِّنْهُمْ يَوْمَئِذٍۢ شَأْنٌۭ يُغْنِيهِ
- 80:38 وُجُوهٌۭ يَوْمَئِذٍۢ مُّسْفِرَةٌۭ
- 80:39 ضَاحِكَةٌۭ مُّسْتَبْشِرَةٌۭ
- 80:40 وَوُجُوهٌۭ يَوْمَئِذٍ عَلَيْهَا غَبَرَةٌۭ
- 80:41 تَرْهَقُهَا قَتَرَةٌ
- 80:42 أُو۟لَٰٓئِكَ هُمُ ٱلْكَفَرَةُ ٱلْفَجَرَةُ


===== _commentary/v16/work/s080/surah.r2/dictionary.md =====
# Dictionary: every branch of every focus root

Every root of the surah's words, each once, headed by the ayah and word where it first occurs; it also serves the later words listed with it.
One entry per branch: a Turkish label and Turkish glosses of its attested senses, then the classical dictionaries' own phrases with their source tags (ayn, sihah, tahdhib, maqayis, mufradat, jamhara). [kalıp] marks a sense the dictionaries attest only inside a fixed expression.
Identity roots come from the quran-data gateway. Word-scoped alternatives are cited analyses of this
exact word. **Echo roots** are observed but withheld mappings: sound-family candidates, not identity.

## ع ب س (root_000975): 80:1 عَبَسَ

- **B001** öfke ya da sıkıntıyla yüzünü çatmak — öfke ya da sıkıntıyla yüzünü çatmak · yüzünü iyice çatıp sevimsizleştirmek · yüz çatma; asık yüzlülük · yüzünü asıp çatma · asık yüzlü ve öfkeli · çok sık yüzünü asan kimse
  عبس يعبس عبوسا فهو عابس الوجه غضبان (ayn;maqayis)؛ عبس الرجل يعبس عبسا وعبوسا إذا قطب وجهه (jamhara)؛ عبس الرجل يعبس عبوسا كلح والتعبس التجهم (sihah)؛ يقال للرجل إذا قطب ما بين عينيه عبس يعبس عبوسا فهو عابس وعبس تعبيسا إذا كره وجهه (tahdhib)؛ العبوس قطوب الوجه من ضيق الصدر (mufradat)
- **B002** çetin ve kasvetli gün [kalıp] — insanlara ağır gelen çetin ve kasvetli gün
  ثم اشتق من هذا اليوم العبوس وهو الشديد الكريه (maqayis)؛ يوم عبوس أي شديد (sihah;tahdhib)؛ يوما عبوسا قمطريرا (mufradat)
- **B003** devenin üzerinde kuruyan idrar ve dışkı tabakası — devenin kuyruğuna ve butlarına yapışıp kuruyan idrar ve dışkı tabakası · develerin üzerindeki idrar ve dışkının kuruyup yapışması · develerin kurumuş idrar ve dışkı tabakasıyla kaplanması · kirin elde veya benzeri bir yerde kuruması
  العَبْس ما يبس على هلب الذنب من البعر والبول (ayn;maqayis)؛ العَبْس ما لصق بأوراك الإبل من خطرها بأذنابها (jamhara)؛ العَبْس ما يتعلق في أذناب الإبل من أبوالها وأبعارها فيجف عليها (sihah)؛ أن تجف أبوالها وأبعارها على أفخاذها وذلك العَبْس (tahdhib)؛ عبس الوسخ في يد فلان أي يبس (sihah)
- **B004** yüz çatma ve korkutucu heybet çağrışımlı aslan adları — yüz çatmayla ilişkilendirilen bir aslan adı · öteki aslanları kaçıracak kadar heybetli aslan · aslan adıyla adlandırılmış bir topluluk
  العنبس اسم من أسماء الأسد والنون زائدة فيه وهو من تقطيب الوجه (jamhara)؛ العنبس الأسد وهو فنعل من العبوس (sihah)؛ العباس الأسد الذي تهرب منه الأسد وبه سمي الرجل عباسا (tahdhib)؛ العنبس من أسماء الأسد وهذا مما زيدت فيه النون وهو فنعل من العبوس (maqayis)
- **B005** kişi adları ve bir Arap kabilesinin adı — bu söz ailesinden türemiş Arap kişi adları · belirli bir Arap kabilesinin ve kurucu atasının adı
  وقد سمت العرب عباسا وعابسا وعبسا وعبيسا وبنو عبس قبيلة منهم (jamhara)؛ عبس أبو قبيلة من قيس (sihah)؛ عبس قبيلة من قيس عيلان وعبيس اسم وعباس اسم (tahdhib)
- **B006** belirli bir bitkinin adı — sözlükte bu adla anılan belirli bir bitki
  العَبْس ضرب من النبت يسمى بالفارسية شابابك والسيسنبر (jamhara)

- **B005** özel yakınlık ve bağlılık bağı — özgür bırakan, özgür bırakılan, soy yakını veya komşu gibi bağlı kişi · özgür bırakma ilişkisine bağlı özel hak ve mensubiyet · soy yakınları veya özgür bırakma bağıyla bağlı kişiler · nimet veya özgür bırakma bağı kuran kişi

- **B006** yüzünü veya dikkatini yöneltme — yüzünü bir şeye çevirmek · yüzünü o yöne dönmüş veya ona uyan kişi · kulağını veya dikkatini bir şeye vermek

- **B010** önceki yağmuru izleyen yağmur — önceki yağmurdan sonra gelen yağmur · erken mevsim yağmurunu izleyen yağmur adı · toprağa izleyen yağmurun yağması · iyilik ardından gelen yağmur veya iyilik
  الولي المطر الذي يكون بعد الوسمي؛ وليت الأرض وليا فهي مولية (ayn)؛ الولي المطر يجيء بعد الوسمي سمي بذلك لأنه يلي الوسمي (maqayis)؛ الولي المطرة بعد الوسمي؛ وليت الأرض فهي مولية (jamhara)؛ الولي المطر بعد الوسمي؛ وليت الأرض وليا (sihah)؛ الولي المطر الذي يأتي بعد المطر؛ وليت الأرض وليا؛ أمطرني ولية منك (tahdhib)
- **B011** deve sırtı alt örtüsü — deve sırtında semer altında kullanılan örtü · semer altı örtüleri
  الولية الحلس والولايا جمعه (ayn)؛ الولية شبيهة بالبرذعة تطرح على ظهر البعير؛ الجمع ولايا (jamhara)؛ الولية البرذعة؛ التي تكون تحت البرذعة؛ الجمع الولايا (sihah)؛ الولية البرذعة وجمعها الولايا؛ البرذعة التي تحت الرحل (tahdhib)
- **B012** ele geçirip hedefe ulaşma [kalıp] — bir şeyi ele geçirmek veya ona üstün gelmek · hedefe varmak veya ona önce ulaşmak

- **B013** birine iyi ya da kötü şey yöneltme [kalıp] — birine iyilik yapmak veya bir şeyi ona ulaştırmak · birine iyilik veya kötülük yöneltmek

  والتطهر التنزه عن الذم وكل قبيح (maqayis)؛ التطهر أيضا التنزه والكف عن الإثم (ayn)؛ يتطهرون أي يتنزهون من الادناس ورجل طاهر الثياب أي متنزه (sihah)؛ التطهر التنزه عن الإثم وما لا يحمد (tahdhib)؛ التاركين للذنب والعاملين للصلاح (mufradat)

  انفجر عليهم القوم وانفجرت عليهم الدواهي إذا جاءهم الكثير منها بغتة (ayn)؛ انفجرت عليهم الدواهي إذا جاءهم الكثير منها بغته (tahdhib)
- **B004** doğruluk sınırını çiğneyerek kötülüğe sapma — taşkın kötülük, başkaldırı ve yalan · doğru yoldan sapıp kötülüklere daldı · yalan söyledi · yalan söyledi, cinsel sınırı çiğnedi ya da inancı reddetti · doğru yoldan sapmış kimse · terkin oturma yeri yana yatıktır · kötülük, kuşku ve yalan · ey doğru yoldan sapmış kadın · sana yalan söyleyen, karşı gelen ya da sözünden çıkan kişi
  الانبعاث والتفتح في المعاصي فجورا (maqayis)؛ سمي الكذب فجورا (maqayis)؛ كل مائل عن الحق فاجر (maqayis)؛ الفجور الريبة والكذب (ayn;tahdhib)؛ انبعاثه في المعاصي (jamhara)؛ فجر فجورا أي فسق وفجر أي كذب وأصله الميل (sihah)؛ الفجور شق ستر الديانة (mufradat)؛ سمي الكاذب فاجرا لكون الكذب بعض الفجور (mufradat)؛ أفجر إذا كذب وأفجر إذا عصى بفرجه وأفجر إذا كفر (tahdhib)
- **B005** taşarcasına bol iyilik ve eli açıklık — bol iyilik ve eli açıklık · iyiliği ve yardımı · iyiliği taşarcasına bol kimse · iyiliğin taşıp yayılması · çokça varlık getirdi
  الفجر وهو الكرم والتفجر بالخير (maqayis)؛ وما أكثر فجره أي معروفه (ayn)؛ رجل ذو فجر إذا كان يتفجر بالخير (jamhara)؛ الفجر الكرم والتفجر في الخير (sihah)؛ الفجر الجود الواسع والكرم (tahdhib)؛ أفجر الرجل إذا جاء بالفجر وهو المال الكثير (tahdhib)
- **B006** dokunulmazlığın çiğnenmesiyle adlandırılan belirli savaş günleri — dokunulmazlığın çiğnendiği belirli savaş günleri
  يوم الفجار يوم للعرب استحلت فيه الحرمة (maqayis)؛ انفجار من وقعات العرب بعكاظ (ayn)؛ أيام الفجار أربعة أفجرة (jamhara;sihah)؛ وإنما سمت قريش هذه الحرب فجارا لأنها كانت في الأشهر الحرم (sihah)؛ أيام الفجار أيام وقائع كانت بعكاظ واستحلوا الحرمات (tahdhib)؛ أيام الفجار وقائع اشتدت بين العرب (mufradat)



===== _commentary/v16/work/s080/surah.r2/channels.md =====
(source: latent_activation/network/v3/reviews/s080/reader_a_pilot.md)

# S080 Semantic Channel Discovery

## Parent Channels

### 1. Directed Awareness and Cognitive Response
- Semantic invariant: A subject opens, directs, withholds, or loses perceptual and cognitive access to an object.
- Surface relation: direct; 80:1-12 turns on attention, blindness, fear, reminder, and distraction, while 80:24 commands reflective looking.
- Surprising reach: The same system extends from eyes and ears into sentinels, debate, impaired hearts, and total self-surrender.

#### Subchannel A. Seeing, Gaze, and Occlusion
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A viewer directs sight or reflection toward an object while blindness, distance, or concealment can block access.
- Active motifs: directed seeing and reflection `ن ظ ر:B001/m01`; intense gaze `ح د ق:B004/m01`; sensory detection `ء ن س:B002/m01`; bodily blindness `ع م ي:B001/m01`; blindness of the heart `ع م ي:B002/m01`; far sight `ش ي ء:B003/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `ن ظ ر` 80:24 `يَنظُرِ`; `ح د ق` 80:30 `حَدَائِقَ`; `ء ن س` 80:17,24 `إِنسَانُ`; `ع م ي` 80:2 `أَعْمَىٰ`; `ش ي ء` 80:12,18,22 `شَاءَ/شَىْءٍ`.
- Synthesis: Directed sight, inward reflection, and long-range seeing form one access scene whose negative forms are physical blindness and the heart's inability to apprehend.

#### Subchannel B. Listening and Acoustic Impact
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Sound is produced, propagated, and received with effects ranging from recognition to overwhelming impact.
- Active motifs: intentional listening `ش ي ء:B005/m01`; deafening cry `ص خ خ:B001/m01`; reverberating echo `ص د ي:B004/m01`; raised voice `ر ف ع:B010/m01`; loud speech or noise `ب ر ر:B004/m01`; sensory detection by sound `ء ن س:B002/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `ش ي ء` 80:12,18,22 `شَاءَ/شَىْءٍ`; `ص خ خ` 80:33 `الصَّاخَّةُ`; `ص د ي` 80:6 `تَصَدَّىٰ`; `ر ف ع` 80:14 `مَرْفُوعَةٍ`; `ب ر ر` 80:16 `بَرَرَةٍ`; `ء ن س` 80:17,24 `إِنسَانُ`.
- Synthesis: Listening occupies the receptive end of a scene that includes loud utterance, echo, and the cry whose intensity defeats ordinary hearing.

#### Subchannel C. Recollection, Reminder, and Forgetting
- Reading type: surface-primary
- Scene or process: A remembered matter is called back, voiced as a reminder, or lost through forgetting and neglect.
- Active motifs: inward recollection `ذ ك ر:B003/m01`; spoken mention `ذ ك ر:B004/m01`; reminder `ذ ك ر:B009/m01`; forgetting `ء م ه:B001/m01`; deliberate neglect `ش ء ن:B006/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `ذ ك ر` 80:4,11,12 `يَذَّكَّرُ/تَذْكِرَةٌ/ذَكَرَ`; `ء م ه` 80:35 `أُمِّ`; `ش ء ن` 80:37 `شَأْنٌ`.
- Synthesis: Memory becomes a communicative process: an absent matter is recovered, mentioned, and made useful as reminder, while forgetting and disregard interrupt the sequence.

#### Subchannel D. Distraction and Cognitive Dimming
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Attention turns away until awareness is dulled, obscured, or suspended.
- Active motifs: diversion from a concern `ل ه و:B001/m01`; heedless preoccupation `ل ه ي:B001/m01`; a deadened heart `م و ت:B006/m01`; a water-like dull heart `م و ه:B008/m01`; involuntary derangement `ء ر ض:B012/m01`; fainting or madness `م و ت:B009/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `ل ه و` 80:10 `تَلَهَّىٰ`; `م و ت` 80:21 `أَمَاتَ`; `م و ه` 80:25 `مَاءً`; `ء ر ض` 80:26 `أَرْضَ`.
- Synthesis: Ordinary distraction grades into figurative cardiac dullness and finally into states where cognition or voluntary control is suspended.

#### Subchannel E. Watchfulness and Deliberation
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: An observer watches, compares alternatives, and forms a plan before acting.
- Active motifs: vigilant custodian `ن ظ ر:B009/m01`; mutual deliberation `ن ظ ر:B010/m01`; counsel over an affair `ء م ر:B007/m01`; calculation and planning `ق د ر:B005/m01`; estimation before action `خ ل ق:B001/m01`; selective choosing `ن خ ل:B003/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `ن ظ ر` 80:24 `يَنظُرِ`; `ء م ر` 80:23 `أَمَرَهُ`; `ق د ر` 80:19 `قَدَّرَهُ`; `خ ل ق` 80:18-19 `خَلَقَهُ`; `ن خ ل` 80:29 `نَخْلًا`.
- Synthesis: Watching is not passive: it supports comparison, counsel, prior estimation, selection, and the conversion of alternatives into an intended plan.

### 2. Intention, Motion, and Encounter
- Semantic invariant: A participant selects a goal, follows a course, and changes relation to others through approach, arrival, pursuit, or withdrawal.
- Surface relation: direct; 80:1-10 stages turning away, coming, striving, and attentive approach, while 80:20 gives the facilitated path and 80:34 the act of flight.
- Surprising reach: Paths become offspring-like branches, repeated arrival becomes dominance, and bodily facing becomes social alignment.

#### Subchannel A. Aim, Choice, and Destination
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: An agent singles out a goal and directs readiness or movement toward it.
- Active motifs: aiming and readiness `ء ب ب:B002/m01`; intentional selection `ء خ و:B003/m01`; directed aim `ء م م:B012/m01`; targeting a raid `د ر ي:B002/m01`; intended concern `ش ء ن:B002/m01`; choosing by sifting `ن خ ل:B003/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `ء ب ب` 80:31 `أَبًّا`; `ء خ و` 80:34 `أَخِيهِ`; `ء م م` 80:35 `أُمِّهِ`; `د ر ي` 80:3 `يُدْرِيكَ`; `ش ء ن` 80:37 `شَأْنٌ`; `ن خ ل` 80:29 `نَخْلًا`.
- Synthesis: Readiness, selection, targeting, and concern are successive aspects of one directed act: choosing what matters and orienting action toward it.

#### Subchannel B. Road, Travel, and the Wayfarer
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A traveler enters a marked way, traverses distance, and may continue without pause.
- Active motifs: road or course `س ب ل:B001/m01`; wayfarer `س ب ل:B002/m01`; travel `س ف ر:B003/m01`; ascent or onward travel `ر ف ع:B011/m01`; uninterrupted travel `ص ب ب:B010/m01`; remote distance `ش ق ق:B005/m01`; branching minor roads `ب ن ي:B008/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `س ب ل` 80:20 `السَّبِيلَ`; `س ف ر` 80:15,38 `سَفَرَةٍ/مُسْفِرَةٌ`; `ر ف ع` 80:14 `مَرْفُوعَةٍ`; `ص ب ب` 80:25 `صَبَبْنَا`; `ش ق ق` 80:26 `شَقَقْنَا`; `ب ن ي` 80:36 `بَنِيهِ`.
- Synthesis: The route scene joins the traversable way, its traveler, its branching tracks, distance, ascent, and sustained movement.

#### Subchannel C. Coming, Bringing, and Presentation
- Reading type: surface-primary
- Scene or process: A person or thing arrives, is brought forward, or is formally presented to an authority.
- Active motifs: coming or arrival `ج ي ء:B001/m01`; bringing or presenting `ج ي ء:B004/m01`; formal presentation to authority `ر ف ع:B004/m01`; confronting face-to-face `و ج ه:B003/m01`; exposing oneself before another `ء ر ض:B007/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `ج ي ء` 80:2,8,33 `جَاءَهُ/جَاءَتِ`; `ر ف ع` 80:14 `مَرْفُوعَةٍ`; `و ج ه` 80:38,40 `وُجُوهٌ`; `ء ر ض` 80:26 `أَرْضَ`.
- Synthesis: Ordinary arrival develops into causative bringing and formal presentation, with confrontation and exposure marking the endpoint of approach.

#### Subchannel D. Nearness, Facing, and Accompaniment
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Participants enter proximity, turn toward one another, and establish accompaniment or association.
- Active motifs: immediate proximity `و ل ي:B001/m01`; turning the face or senses toward something `و ل ي:B006/m01`; frontal nearness `ء م م:B011/m01`; catching up and drawing near `ر ه ق:B002/m01`; accompaniment `ء ل ي:B003/m01`; companionship `ص ح ب:B001/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `و ل ي` 80:1 `تَوَلَّىٰ`; `ء م م` 80:35 `أُمِّهِ`; `ر ه ق` 80:41 `تَرْهَقُهَا`; `ء ل ي` 80:24 `إِلَىٰ`; `ص ح ب` 80:36 `صَاحِبَتِهِ`.
- Synthesis: Spatial nearness becomes relational: catching up, facing, accompanying, and remaining with another all reduce the interval between participants.

#### Subchannel E. Flight, Pursuit, and Competitive Motion
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Urgent movement separates participants through flight, pursuit, racing, or dispersal.
- Active motifs: flight from another `ف ر ر:B001/m01`; racing and overcoming `س ع ي:B008/m01`; rout by a charge `ء ب ب:B007/m01`; rapid agile gait `ي س ر:B005/m01`; collective dispersal `ن ع م:B008/m01`; dominance through repeated coming `ج ي ء:B001/m02`.
- Ayah anchors: `ف ر ر` 80:34 `يَفِرُّ`; `س ع ي` 80:8 `يَسْعَىٰ`; `ء ب ب` 80:31 `أَبًّا`; `ي س ر` 80:20 `يَسَّرَهُ`; `ن ع م` 80:32 `أَنْعَامِكُمْ`; `ج ي ء` 80:2,8,33 `جَاءَهُ/جَاءَتِ`.
- Synthesis: Flight and dispersal pull bodies apart, while pursuit, racing, and repeated arrival reverse the relation by closing distance and establishing superiority.

### 3. Measure, Capacity, Order, and Control
- Semantic invariant: Possibilities are bounded, compared, ordered, authorized, and converted into executable action.
- Surface relation: direct; 80:19 presents determination and proportion, 80:20 facilitation, 80:23 an unfulfilled command, and 80:37 an all-absorbing affair.
- Surprising reach: Abstract control is repeatedly embodied in the hand, fitted equipment, bonds, and orderly succession.

#### Subchannel A. Estimation, Proportion, and Fit
- Reading type: surface-primary
- Scene or process: An intended result is measured in advance and its parts are fitted to a suitable proportion.
- Active motifs: measure and limit `ق د ر:B001/m01`; planning by calculation `ق د ر:B005/m01`; proportion and fit `ق د ر:B006/m01`; pre-action estimation `خ ل ق:B001/m01`; judgment of value `ط ع م:B008/m01`; aptitude or suitability `خ ل ق:B005/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `ق د ر` 80:19 `قَدَّرَهُ`; `خ ل ق` 80:18-19 `خَلَقَهُ`; `ط ع م` 80:24 `طَعَامِهِ`.
- Synthesis: Estimation supplies limits, proportion fits components, aptitude tests the recipient, and valuation determines whether the projected arrangement is appropriate.

#### Subchannel B. Ability, Ease, and Effort
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: An agent's capacity meets a task that may be facilitated, resisted, or completed through exertion.
- Active motifs: power or possession `ق د ر:B003/m01`; ability `ء ل ي:B011/m01`; ability to perform `ط ع م:B011/m01`; ease after difficulty `ي س ر:B001/m01`; suitability `ء ر ض:B003/m01`; exertion `ء ل ي:B010/m01`; hardship `ش ق ق:B003/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `ق د ر` 80:19 `قَدَّرَهُ`; `ء ل ي` 80:24 `إِلَىٰ`; `ط ع م` 80:24 `طَعَامِهِ`; `ي س ر` 80:20 `يَسَّرَهُ`; `ء ر ض` 80:26 `أَرْضَ`; `ش ق ق` 80:26 `شَقَقْنَا`.
- Synthesis: Capability is relational rather than absolute: a task must suit the agent, become accessible, and still receive the effort needed to overcome hardship.

#### Subchannel C. Parts, Counterparts, and Succession
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Units are paired, divided, matched, or arranged one after another.
- Active motifs: even pair `ز ك و:B005/m01`; half or side `ش ق ق:B002/m01`; totality or whole `ك ل ل:B003/m01`; matching counterpart `ن ظ ر:B006/m01`; uninterrupted succession `و ل ي:B002/m01`; sequential formation `ط ع م:B014/m01`; what lies before `ي د ي:B008/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `ز ك و` 80:3,7 `يَزَّكَّىٰ`; `ش ق ق` 80:26 `شَقَقْنَا`; `ك ل ل` 80:37 `كُلِّ`; `ن ظ ر` 80:24 `يَنظُرِ`; `و ل ي` 80:1 `تَوَلَّىٰ`; `ط ع م` 80:24 `طَعَامِهِ`; `ي د ي` 80:15 `أَيْدِي`.
- Synthesis: Pair, half, whole, equivalent, predecessor, and successor form a relational calculus for locating each unit within a structured series.

#### Subchannel D. Command, Governance, and Judgment
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: An authority formulates an order, administers an affair, and renders a binding decision.
- Active motifs: command or obligation `ء م ر:B002/m01`; governance `ء م ر:B003/m01`; administrative charge `س ع ي:B003/m01`; entrusted rule `و ل ي:B003/m01`; judgment `ق ض ي:B001/m01`; sovereign hand `ي د ي:B005/m01`; presentation to authority `ر ف ع:B004/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `ء م ر` 80:23 `أَمَرَهُ`; `س ع ي` 80:8 `يَسْعَىٰ`; `و ل ي` 80:1 `تَوَلَّىٰ`; `ق ض ي` 80:23 `يَقْضِ`; `ي د ي` 80:15 `أَيْدِي`; `ر ف ع` 80:14 `مَرْفُوعَةٍ`.
- Synthesis: Governance links deliberated command, delegated administration, formal presentation, and judgment under the effective hand of authority.

#### Subchannel E. Coercion, Submission, and Bound Labor
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Force constrains another's will, producing obedience, bondage, labor, or eventual release.
- Active motifs: compulsion `ج ي ء:B005/m01`; coercion into disobedience `ك ف ر:B007/m01`; submissive giving of the hand `ي د ي:B006/m01`; obedience `ص ح ب:B003/m01`; bodily gesture of submission `ك ف ر:B014/m01`; fetter `ص ب ب:B009/m01`; labor toward manumission `س ع ي:B005/m01`; forced prostitution of bondmaids `س ع ي:B007/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `ج ي ء` 80:2,8,33 `جَاءَهُ/جَاءَتِ`; `ك ف ر` 80:17,42 `أَكْفَرَهُ/كَفَرَةٌ`; `ي د ي` 80:15 `أَيْدِي`; `ص ح ب` 80:36 `صَاحِبَتِهِ`; `ص ب ب` 80:25 `صَبَبْنَا`; `س ع ي` 80:8 `يَسْعَىٰ`.
- Synthesis: Constraint ranges from compelled conduct and symbolic submission to literal fettering and exploitative labor, with manumission providing the scene's release outcome.

### 4. Human Formation, Character, and Life Cycle
- Semantic invariant: Human existence is shaped from origin through bodily formation, social maturation, decline, death, and renewed life.
- Surface relation: direct; 80:17-23 explicitly sequences humanity, creation, determination, the path, death, burial, and possible resurrection.
- Surprising reach: Biological formation extends into character, social acceptability, marriage, bereavement, and the built constitution of the body.

#### Subchannel A. Creation and Bodily Constitution
- Reading type: surface-primary
- Scene or process: A human entity is brought into being and given an organized bodily form.
- Active motifs: origination `خ ل ق:B002/m01`; well-proportioned form `خ ل ق:B003/m01`; bodily constitution `ب ن ي:B002/m01`; stature and form `ء م م:B006/m01`; human person `م ر ء:B001/m01`; mankind `ب ش ر:B002/m01`; humanity `ء ن س:B001/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `خ ل ق` 80:18-19 `خَلَقَهُ`; `ب ن ي` 80:36 `بَنِيهِ`; `ء م م` 80:35 `أُمِّهِ`; `م ر ء` 80:34,37 `الْمَرْءُ/امْرِئٍ`; `ب ش ر` 80:39 `مُسْتَبْشِرَةٌ`; `ء ن س` 80:17,24 `الْإِنسَانُ`.
- Synthesis: Creation is rendered as organized construction: humanity receives stature, proportion, bodily constitution, and the status of an individuated person.

#### Subchannel B. Character, Virtue, and Disfigurement
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Outer form and inner disposition are evaluated as sound, virtuous, distorted, or defective.
- Active motifs: moral character `خ ل ق:B004/m01`; complete outer and inner virtue `ب ش ر:B008/m01`; acceptable personhood `م ر ء:B006/m01`; bodily or moral disfigurement `ش ي ء:B002/m01`; human defect `ء م م:B015/m01`; forged characterization `خ ل ق:B007/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `خ ل ق` 80:18-19 `خَلَقَهُ`; `ب ش ر` 80:39 `مُسْتَبْشِرَةٌ`; `م ر ء` 80:34,37 `الْمَرْءُ/امْرِئٍ`; `ش ي ء` 80:12,18,22 `شَاءَ/شَىْءٍ`; `ء م م` 80:35 `أُمِّهِ`.
- Synthesis: The human form becomes an ethical surface: constitution and character can be completed, accepted, falsified in description, or marred.

#### Subchannel C. Reproduction, Sex, and Birth
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Sexual union and generative fluid lead to gendered embodiment and birth.
- Active motifs: seminal fluid `ن ط ف:B002/m03`; semen as water `م و ه:B004/m01`; male sex `ذ ك ر:B001/m01`; phallus `ق ض ب:B009/m01`; consummated union `ب ن ي:B006/m01`; marriage `غ ن ي:B006/m01`; hands-first birth `و ج ه:B010/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `ن ط ف` 80:19 `نُطْفَةٍ`; `م و ه` 80:25 `مَاءً`; `ذ ك ر` 80:4,11,12 `يَذَّكَّرُ/تَذْكِرَةٌ/ذَكَرَ`; `ق ض ب` 80:28 `قَضْبًا`; `ب ن ي` 80:36 `بَنِيهِ`; `غ ن ي` 80:5,37 `اسْتَغْنَىٰ/يُغْنِيهِ`; `و ج ه` 80:38,40 `وُجُوهٌ`.
- Synthesis: The lexical scene joins generative fluid, male anatomy, union, marriage, and the physical manner of emergence at birth.

#### Subchannel D. Upbringing, Youth, Adulthood, and Aging
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A dependent human is raised, grows into companionship, and eventually enters decline.
- Active motifs: human upbringing as cultivation `ن ب ت:B006/m01`; youth and demographic growth `ن ب ت:B003/m01`; fatherly nurture `ء ب و:B001/m01`; adult child becoming a companion `ص ح ب:B005/m01`; aging and decline `و ج ه:B009/m01`; bereaved parent `م و ت:B005/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `ن ب ت` 80:27 `أَنْبَتْنَا`; `ء ب و` 80:35 `أَبِيهِ`; `ص ح ب` 80:36 `صَاحِبَتِهِ`; `و ج ه` 80:38,40 `وُجُوهٌ`; `م و ت` 80:21 `أَمَاتَهُ`.
- Synthesis: Cultivation supplies the governing analogy for nurture and youth; maturity changes child into companion, while aging and bereavement expose the fragility of the family line.

#### Subchannel E. Death, Burial, and Revival
- Reading type: surface-primary
- Scene or process: Life ends, the body is placed in the grave, and revivification remains possible.
- Active motifs: death `م و ت:B001/m01`; judicial or appointed death `ق ض ي:B003/m01`; burial `ق ب ر:B001/m01`; killing `ق ت ل:B001/m01`; bodily weakening toward death `م و ت:B002/m01`; revivification `ن ش ر:B002/m01`; carrion `م و ت:B007/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `م و ت` 80:21 `أَمَاتَهُ`; `ق ض ي` 80:23 `يَقْضِ`; `ق ب ر` 80:21 `أَقْبَرَهُ`; `ق ت ل` 80:17 `قُتِلَ`; `ن ش ر` 80:22 `أَنْشَرَهُ`.
- Synthesis: Killing or weakening terminates embodied life, burial encloses the remains, and revival reverses the closure by restoring animation.

### 5. Kinship, Community, and Social Continuity
- Semantic invariant: Persons acquire identity, obligation, and support through descent, companionship, guardianship, and inherited collective forms.
- Surface relation: direct; 80:34-37 enumerates brother, mother, father, spouse, children, and the isolating affair of each person.
- Surprising reach: Kinship patterns recur in plant offshoots, branch roads, clientage, religious tradition, and political leadership.

#### Subchannel A. Parent, Mother, and Origin
- Reading type: surface-primary
- Scene or process: A parent nurtures a descendant and also serves as a figure for source, center, and origin.
- Active motifs: fatherhood and nurture `ء ب و:B001/m01`; mother `ء م م:B001/m01`; mother `ء م ه:B004/m01`; source and central origin `ء م م:B002/m01`; origin of growth `ن ب ت:B005/m01`; filial vocative `ء ب و:B002/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `ء ب و` 80:35 `أَبِيهِ`; `ء م م` 80:35 `أُمِّهِ`; `ن ب ت` 80:27 `أَنْبَتْنَا`.
- Synthesis: Literal parenthood and the wider source relation share a generative center from which persons, growth, and collective identity proceed.

#### Subchannel B. Children, Descent, and Clan
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Descendants extend a line that becomes a named household, lineage, or clan.
- Active motifs: children and lineage `ب ن ي:B007/m01`; clan name `ن ب ت:B004/m01`; tribal name `ع ب س:B005/m01`; clan affiliation `ف ر ر:B014/m01`; collateral kin and inheritance `ك ل ل:B004/m01`; offspring-like branch roads `ب ن ي:B008/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `ب ن ي` 80:36 `بَنِيهِ`; `ن ب ت` 80:27 `أَنْبَتْنَا`; `ع ب س` 80:1 `عَبَسَ`; `ف ر ر` 80:34 `يَفِرُّ`; `ك ل ل` 80:37 `كُلِّ`.
- Synthesis: Descent expands from children into collateral relatives and named clans, while branching roads preserve the same source-to-offshoot geometry.

#### Subchannel C. Sibling, Companion, and Intimate
- Reading type: surface-primary
- Scene or process: Social nearness is formed through siblinghood, familiarity, companionship, and affection.
- Active motifs: sibling and close friend `ء خ و:B001/m01`; companionship `ص ح ب:B001/m01`; familiarity `ء ن س:B003/m01`; favored intimate `ء ن س:B006/m01`; love `ح ب ب:B002/m01`; affectionate alliance `و ل ي:B004/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `ء خ و` 80:34 `أَخِيهِ`; `ص ح ب` 80:36 `صَاحِبَتِهِ`; `ء ن س` 80:17,24 `إِنسَانُ`; `ح ب ب` 80:27 `حَبًّا`; `و ل ي` 80:1 `تَوَلَّىٰ`.
- Synthesis: Blood siblinghood and chosen intimacy converge in a relation of familiarity, affection, accompaniment, and mutual support.

#### Subchannel D. Care, Protection, and Guardianship
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A stronger party assumes responsibility for a dependent, relative, neighbor, or client.
- Active motifs: care for kin `ب ر ر:B003/m01`; protective companionship `ص ح ب:B002/m01`; guardianship and wardship `و ل ي:B003/m01`; aid and defense `ي د ي:B016/m01`; clientage and legal kinship `و ل ي:B005/m01`; dependent burden `ك ل ل:B002/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `ب ر ر` 80:16 `بَرَرَةٍ`; `ص ح ب` 80:36 `صَاحِبَتِهِ`; `و ل ي` 80:1 `تَوَلَّىٰ`; `ي د ي` 80:15 `أَيْدِي`; `ك ل ل` 80:37 `كُلِّ`.
- Synthesis: Care binds protector and dependent through companionship, guardianship, defense, clientage, and the practical burden of support.

#### Subchannel E. Community, Tradition, and Leadership
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Individuals gather into a people whose inherited way is embodied and directed by an exemplar.
- Active motifs: community or species `ء م م:B004/m01`; religion or inherited tradition `ء م م:B005/m01`; leader and exemplar `ء م م:B009/m01`; natural or unlettered condition `ء م م:B007/m01`; collective mixing `ف ر ر:B016/m01`; clustered groups `ك ل ل:B009/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `ء م م` 80:35 `أُمِّهِ`; `ف ر ر` 80:34 `يَفِرُّ`; `ك ل ل` 80:37 `كُلِّ`.
- Synthesis: Community is a collected human body with a received tradition, a guiding exemplar, and degrees of mixture or subgrouping.

### 6. Land, Water, and Vegetative Renewal
- Semantic invariant: Terrain receives water, opens, and becomes a productive field of germination, growth, and enclosure.
- Surface relation: direct; 80:24-32 traces food through poured water, split earth, grain, grapes, fodder, olives, palms, gardens, fruit, pasture, and animal provision.
- Surprising reach: The ecological cycle reaches basins, wells, bubbles, dripping surfaces, snowfall, sifting, and offspring-like saplings.

#### Subchannel A. Earth, Soil, and Pasture
- Reading type: surface-primary
- Scene or process: Land provides a low, receptive, and fertile substrate in which growth can remain rooted.
- Active motifs: fertile soft earth `ء ر ض:B002/m01`; low ground `ء ر ض:B001/m01`; remaining attached to the ground `ء ر ض:B006/m01`; fertile soil and rain `ك ر م:B002/m01`; open land or desert `ب ر ر:B005/m01`; pasture `ء ب ب:B001/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `ء ر ض` 80:26 `أَرْضَ`; `ك ر م` 80:13,16 `مُكَرَّمَةٍ/كِرَامٍ`; `ب ر ر` 80:16 `بَرَرَةٍ`; `ء ب ب` 80:31 `أَبًّا`.
- Synthesis: Low and fertile ground receives growth, supports pasture, and holds organisms in place, joining terrain and productivity in one substrate scene.

#### Subchannel B. Pouring, Dripping, and Collection
- Reading type: surface-primary
- Scene or process: Water descends, runs, ripples, and collects in hollows or vessels.
- Active motifs: water `م و ه:B001/m01`; abundance of water `م و ه:B002/m01`; pouring `ص ب ب:B001/m01`; dripping and prolonged wetness `ن ط ف:B003/m01`; bubbles and ripples `ح ب ب:B008/m01`; basin or depression `ج ي ء:B002/m01`; water-holding hollow `خ ل ق:B011/m01`; shallow pool `ص ح ف:B004/m02`.
- Ayah anchors: `م و ه` 80:25 `مَاءً`; `ص ب ب` 80:25 `صَبَبْنَا`; `ن ط ف` 80:19 `نُطْفَةٍ`; `ح ب ب` 80:27 `حَبًّا`; `ج ي ء` 80:2,8,33 `جَاءَهُ/جَاءَتِ`; `خ ل ق` 80:18-19 `خَلَقَهُ`; `ص ح ف` 80:13 `صُحُفٍ`.
- Synthesis: Falling and running water is tracked through droplets, surface motion, and eventual containment in basin, pool, or excavated hollow.

#### Subchannel C. Rain, Irrigation, and Revival
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Precipitation reaches the land, irrigation distributes it, and dormant vegetation revives.
- Active motifs: rain `س ب ل:B005/m01`; later seasonal rain `و ل ي:B010/m01`; snow or rain `ن خ ل:B004/m01`; irrigation `م و ه:B003/m01`; rain reviving herbage `ن ش ر:B004/m01`; fissure with gushing water `ف ج ر:B001/m01`; earth splitting open `ش ق ق:B001/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `س ب ل` 80:20 `السَّبِيلَ`; `و ل ي` 80:1 `تَوَلَّىٰ`; `ن خ ل` 80:29 `نَخْلًا`; `م و ه` 80:25 `مَاءً`; `ن ش ر` 80:22 `أَنْشَرَهُ`; `ف ج ر` 80:42 `فَجَرَةٌ`; `ش ق ق` 80:26 `شَقَقْنَا`.
- Synthesis: Rain and irrigation supply water, fissures admit or release it, and vegetal revival displays the productive outcome.

#### Subchannel D. Seed, Germination, and Herbage
- Reading type: surface-primary
- Scene or process: Seed is covered, planted, and brought through the earth as grain, herbage, and fodder.
- Active motifs: grain or seed `ح ب ب:B001/m01`; plant growth `ن ب ت:B001/m01`; planting `ن ب ت:B002/m01`; increase and growth `ز ك و:B001/m01`; farmer covering seed `ك ف ر:B008/m01`; grain spike `س ب ل:B008/m01`; fresh fodder `ق ض ب:B003/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `ح ب ب` 80:27 `حَبًّا`; `ن ب ت` 80:27 `أَنْبَتْنَا`; `ز ك و` 80:3,7 `يَزَّكَّىٰ`; `ك ف ر` 80:17,42 `أَكْفَرَهُ/كَفَرَةٌ`; `س ب ل` 80:20 `السَّبِيلَ`; `ق ض ب` 80:28 `قَضْبًا`.
- Synthesis: The seed is hidden, planted, increased, and differentiated into spike and fodder, forming a complete cultivation process.

#### Subchannel E. Palm, Vine, Grove, and Garden
- Reading type: surface-primary
- Scene or process: Cultivated trees gather into productive, bounded plantings.
- Active motifs: palm tree `ن خ ل:B001/m01`; vine `ك ر م:B004/m01`; olive tree `ز ي ت:B002/m01`; enclosed garden `ح د ق:B003/m01`; small palm `ش ي ء:B006/m01`; tall dry palm `ص د ي:B009/m01`; opening palm spathe `ض ح ك:B007/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `ن خ ل` 80:29 `نَخْلًا`; `ك ر م` 80:13,16 `مُكَرَّمَةٍ/كِرَامٍ`; `ز ي ت` 80:29 `زَيْتُونًا`; `ح د ق` 80:30 `حَدَائِقَ`; `ش ي ء` 80:12,18,22 `شَاءَ/شَىْءٍ`; `ص د ي` 80:6 `تَصَدَّىٰ`; `ض ح ك` 80:39 `ضَاحِكَةٌ`.
- Synthesis: Individual palm, vine, and olive are joined by enclosure into the garden, with sapling, mature trunk, and opening spathe marking stages of arboreal life.

### 7. Harvest, Food, Hospitality, and Husbandry
- Semantic invariant: Productive land is converted into food, bodily nourishment, shared provision, and sustained animal wealth.
- Surface relation: direct; 80:24-32 moves from human food through crops and fruit to pasture and livestock provision.
- Surprising reach: The scene includes cutting tools, sieves, mills, cookware, ceremonial eating, digestion, udder retention, and weaning.

#### Subchannel A. Reaping, Sifting, and Milling
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Mature crop is lifted, cut, separated, and processed into usable grain.
- Active motifs: carrying the harvest `ر ف ع:B006/m01`; wheat `ب ر ر:B006/m01`; cutting crop `ق ض ب:B001/m01`; sword or sickle as cutter `ق ض ب:B005/m01`; sifting `ن خ ل:B002/m01`; grain entering a mill `ل ه و:B003/m01`; spike of grain `س ب ل:B008/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `ر ف ع` 80:14 `مَرْفُوعَةٍ`; `ب ر ر` 80:16 `بَرَرَةٍ`; `ق ض ب` 80:28 `قَضْبًا`; `ن خ ل` 80:29 `نَخْلًا`; `ل ه و` 80:10 `تَلَهَّىٰ`; `س ب ل` 80:20 `السَّبِيلَ`.
- Synthesis: Harvested stalks pass through lifting, cutting, sifting, and milling until the grain is separated from its coverings and ready for use.

#### Subchannel B. Fruit, Ripening, and Oil
- Reading type: surface-primary
- Scene or process: Fruit develops on tree or vine, ripens, and is eaten or pressed into oil.
- Active motifs: grapes `ع ن ب:B001/m01`; fruit `ف ك ه:B002/m01`; olive `ز ي ت:B002/m01`; oil extraction `ز ي ت:B001/m01`; fruit becoming ripe `ط ع م:B005/m01`; fruit covering `ك ف ر:B010/m01`; jujube fruit `ع ن ب:B003/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `ع ن ب` 80:28 `عِنَبًا`; `ف ك ه` 80:31 `فَاكِهَةً`; `ز ي ت` 80:29 `زَيْتُونًا`; `ط ع م` 80:24 `طَعَامِهِ`; `ك ف ر` 80:17,42 `أَكْفَرَهُ/كَفَرَةٌ`.
- Synthesis: The fruit scene follows protected growth through ripening and consumption, with the olive adding extraction as a second path from fruit to provision.

#### Subchannel C. Eating, Digestion, and Nourished Flesh
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Food is tasted, swallowed, digested, and incorporated into the body.
- Active motifs: eating and tasting `ط ع م:B001/m01`; feeding another `ط ع م:B002/m01`; digestibility `م ر ء:B003/m01`; esophagus `م ر ء:B005/m01`; food building flesh `ب ن ي:B010/m01`; fullness from drinking `ح ب ب:B006/m01`; surfeit `ن ط ف:B006/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `ط ع م` 80:24 `طَعَامِهِ`; `م ر ء` 80:34,37 `الْمَرْءُ/امْرِئٍ`; `ب ن ي` 80:36 `بَنِيهِ`; `ح ب ب` 80:27 `حَبًّا`; `ن ط ف` 80:19 `نُطْفَةٍ`.
- Synthesis: Food moves from taste and feeding through the throat and digestion to bodily incorporation, with fullness and surfeit marking the limit of intake.

#### Subchannel D. Host, Guest, and Shared Provision
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A host receives others, apportions food, and converts stored goods into hospitality.
- Active motifs: hospitality and income `ط ع م:B004/m01`; ceremonial eating `م ر ء:B004/m01`; crowding by guests or petitioners `ر ه ق:B006/m01`; commodity or provision `م ت ع:B003/m01`; favorable provision `ن ع م:B001/m01`; reciprocal gift `ك ر م:B008/m01`; food platter `ص ح ف:B004/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `ط ع م` 80:24 `طَعَامِهِ`; `م ر ء` 80:34,37 `الْمَرْءُ/امْرِئٍ`; `ر ه ق` 80:41 `تَرْهَقُهَا`; `م ت ع` 80:32 `مَتَاعًا`; `ن ع م` 80:32 `أَنْعَامِكُمْ`; `ك ر م` 80:13,16 `مُكَرَّمَةٍ/كِرَامٍ`; `ص ح ف` 80:13 `صُحُفٍ`.
- Synthesis: Hospitality joins the arriving crowd, stored provision, serving vessel, ceremonial consumption, and the reciprocal social obligation created by a meal.

#### Subchannel E. Flock, Milk, Grazing, and Weaning
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Herded animals graze, yield milk, reproduce, and are separated from their young under human care.
- Active motifs: livestock wealth `ن ع م:B005/m01`; pasture `ء ب ب:B001/m01`; retaining milk in the udder `ر ف ع:B007/m01`; abundant milk and offspring `ي س ر:B006/m01`; prebirth milk `ف ك ه:B004/m01`; night grazing `ن ش ر:B006/m01`; livestock caretaker `ص د ي:B005/m01`; weaning young animals `و ل ي:B015/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `ن ع م` 80:32 `أَنْعَامِكُمْ`; `ء ب ب` 80:31 `أَبًّا`; `ر ف ع` 80:14 `مَرْفُوعَةٍ`; `ي س ر` 80:20 `يَسَّرَهُ`; `ف ك ه` 80:31 `فَاكِهَةً`; `ن ش ر` 80:22 `أَنْشَرَهُ`; `ص د ي` 80:6 `تَصَدَّىٰ`; `و ل ي` 80:1 `تَوَلَّىٰ`.
- Synthesis: Pasture supports the flock, the caretaker regulates grazing and milk, and weaning converts reproductive abundance into an ordered husbandry cycle.

### 8. Blessing, Wealth, and Social Worth
- Semantic invariant: Goods and favorable states acquire social meaning through bestowal, sufficiency, rank, generosity, and exchange.
- Surface relation: indirect; 80:5 names self-sufficiency, 80:13-16 honored sheets and noble scribes, 80:32 provision, and 80:37 the limits of what can suffice.
- Surprising reach: Worth is embodied in the hand, face, elevated position, fertile soil, oil gifts, and reciprocal hospitality.

#### Subchannel A. Blessing, Gift, and Benefit
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A favorable good is bestowed, received, and made useful to another.
- Active motifs: blessings `ء ل ي:B006/m01`; gift `ء ل ي:B012/m01`; favorable state and benefaction `ن ع م:B001/m01`; bestowed hand or favor `ي د ي:B003/m01`; conferral of good or harm `و ل ي:B013/m01`; benefit `ن ف ع:B001/m01`; gift of oil `ز ي ت:B004/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `ء ل ي` 80:24 `إِلَىٰ`; `ن ع م` 80:32 `أَنْعَامِكُمْ`; `ي د ي` 80:15 `أَيْدِي`; `و ل ي` 80:1 `تَوَلَّىٰ`; `ن ف ع` 80:4 `تَنْفَعَهُ`; `ز ي ت` 80:29 `زَيْتُونًا`.
- Synthesis: Blessing becomes socially effective when a giver places a usable good in another's hand and the recipient derives benefit from it.

#### Subchannel B. Sufficiency, Wealth, and Utility
- Reading type: surface-primary
- Scene or process: Resources relieve need, sustain residence, and provide durable utility or enjoyment.
- Active motifs: wealth and needlessness `غ ن ي:B001/m01`; sufficiency `غ ن ي:B002/m01`; ease and affluence `ي س ر:B003/m01`; utility and enjoyment `م ت ع:B001/m01`; commodity and equipment `م ت ع:B003/m01`; prosperity and increase `ء م ر:B004/m01`; useful quality or strength `م ت ع:B008/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `غ ن ي` 80:5,37 `اسْتَغْنَىٰ/يُغْنِيهِ`; `ي س ر` 80:20 `يَسَّرَهُ`; `م ت ع` 80:32 `مَتَاعًا`; `ء م ر` 80:23 `أَمَرَهُ`.
- Synthesis: Wealth is defined functionally as the capacity of goods, income, and productive increase to remove need and sustain life.

#### Subchannel C. Honor, Rank, and Reputation
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A person is elevated, publicly remembered, and recognized among the leading members of a group.
- Active motifs: honor and generosity `ك ر م:B001/m01`; elevated rank `ر ف ع:B002/m01`; reputation `ذ ك ر:B007/m01`; social standing `و ج ه:B006/m01`; elite status `ف ر ر:B008/m01`; precious person `ك ر م:B010/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `ك ر م` 80:13,16 `مُكَرَّمَةٍ/كِرَامٍ`; `ر ف ع` 80:14 `مَرْفُوعَةٍ`; `ذ ك ر` 80:4,11,12 `يَذَّكَّرُ/تَذْكِرَةٌ/ذَكَرَ`; `و ج ه` 80:38,40 `وُجُوهٌ`; `ف ر ر` 80:34 `يَفِرُّ`.
- Synthesis: Elevation and public mention create rank, while the face of a group and its elite embody collective recognition.

#### Subchannel D. Generosity and Reciprocity
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Giving creates a return relation through generosity, praise, and reciprocal obligation.
- Active motifs: generosity `ف ج ر:B005/m01`; generosity through distribution `ن ش ر:B010/m01`; reciprocal gift `ك ر م:B008/m01`; reciprocity `ص د ي:B011/m01`; gracious praise formula `ك ر م:B009/m01`; praise and desire formula `ح ب ب:B003/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `ف ج ر` 80:42 `فَجَرَةٌ`; `ن ش ر` 80:22 `أَنْشَرَهُ`; `ك ر م` 80:13,16 `مُكَرَّمَةٍ/كِرَامٍ`; `ص د ي` 80:6 `تَصَدَّىٰ`; `ح ب ب` 80:27 `حَبًّا`.
- Synthesis: Generosity spreads goods outward, and reciprocity, praise, and gracious formulae acknowledge or answer the social debt it creates.

#### Subchannel E. Ownership, Payment, and Resale
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Property changes hands through possession, immediate exchange, payment, or transfer at a known price.
- Active motifs: possession `ي د ي:B004/m01`; hand-to-hand payment `ي د ي:B007/m01`; settlement of payment `ق ض ي:B006/m01`; resale at the known purchase price `و ل ي:B014/m01`; commodity `م ت ع:B003/m01`; oil seller or press operator `ز ي ت:B005/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `ي د ي` 80:15 `أَيْدِي`; `ق ض ي` 80:23 `يَقْضِ`; `و ل ي` 80:1 `تَوَلَّىٰ`; `م ت ع` 80:32 `مَتَاعًا`; `ز ي ت` 80:29 `زَيْتُونًا`.
- Synthesis: The economic scene distinguishes holding a good, transferring it directly, discharging its price, and passing it onward through resale or specialized trade.

### 9. Moral Exposure, Purity, and Refusal
- Semantic invariant: Conduct reveals or conceals moral condition through truthfulness, purification, transgression, denial, and submission to obligation.
- Surface relation: direct; 80:3-7 contrasts purification with self-sufficiency, 80:17 and 80:42 name disbelief, and 80:16 and 80:42 oppose the righteous and the transgressing.
- Surprising reach: Ethical states are materialized as washing, seed covering, stains, darkness, fetters, bodily bows, and fulfilled covenants.

#### Subchannel A. Truthfulness, Righteousness, and Sound Character
- Reading type: surface-primary
- Scene or process: A person fulfills truth and embodies an ethically sound disposition.
- Active motifs: truth and fulfilled oath `ب ر ر:B001/m01`; righteousness `ب ر ر:B002/m01`; moral purity `ط ه ر:B005/m01`; pure growth `ز ك و:B002/m01`; ethical character `خ ل ق:B004/m01`; complete virtue `ب ش ر:B008/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `ب ر ر` 80:16 `بَرَرَةٍ`; `ط ه ر` 80:14 `مُطَهَّرَةٍ`; `ز ك و` 80:3,7 `يَزَّكَّىٰ`; `خ ل ق` 80:18-19 `خَلَقَهُ`; `ب ش ر` 80:39 `مُسْتَبْشِرَةٌ`.
- Synthesis: Truth fulfilled in action, purified growth, and stable character converge in the image of a person whose inward disposition and outward conduct agree.

#### Subchannel B. Washing, Purification, and Expiation
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Defilement is removed by washing or a purifying instrument, and moral liability is discharged.
- Active motifs: cleansing `ط ه ر:B001/m01`; washing `ط ه ر:B003/m01`; purifying water or implement `ط ه ر:B004/m01`; unveiling by cleaning `س ف ر:B001/m01`; purity `ز ك و:B002/m01`; expiation `ك ف ر:B009/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `ط ه ر` 80:14 `مُطَهَّرَةٍ`; `س ف ر` 80:15,38 `سَفَرَةٍ/مُسْفِرَةٌ`; `ز ك و` 80:3,7 `يَزَّكَّىٰ`; `ك ف ر` 80:17,42 `أَكْفَرَهُ/كَفَرَةٌ`.
- Synthesis: Physical removal of dirt supplies the mechanism for ethical purification and expiation: an obscuring burden is washed away or discharged.

#### Subchannel C. Covering, Disbelief, and Ingratitude
- Reading type: surface-primary
- Scene or process: A known good or truth is covered, repudiated, or answered with ingratitude.
- Active motifs: literal covering `ك ف ر:B001/m01`; disbelief `ك ف ر:B003/m01`; ingratitude `ك ف ر:B004/m01`; repudiation `ك ف ر:B005/m01`; accusatory denial `ك ف ر:B006/m01`; darkness as covering `ك ف ر:B002/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `ك ف ر` 80:17,42 `أَكْفَرَهُ/كَفَرَةٌ`.
- Synthesis: The root's material act of covering organizes a moral progression from obscuration to repudiation, accusation, and refusal to acknowledge a received good.

#### Subchannel D. Transgression, Stain, and Corruption
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A boundary is crossed and the resulting fault clings to the person or spoils an affair.
- Active motifs: transgression `ف ج ر:B004/m01`; oppressive trespass `ر ه ق:B005/m01`; stain of blame or suspicion `ن ط ف:B004/m01`; spoiling an affair `ش ء ن:B004/m01`; deviation `ش ق ق:B009/m01`; insolence `ف ك ه:B006/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `ف ج ر` 80:42 `فَجَرَةٌ`; `ر ه ق` 80:41 `تَرْهَقُهَا`; `ن ط ف` 80:19 `نُطْفَةٍ`; `ش ء ن` 80:37 `شَأْنٌ`; `ش ق ق` 80:26 `شَقَقْنَا`; `ف ك ه` 80:31 `فَاكِهَةً`.
- Synthesis: Boundary crossing leaves a visible or reputational stain and can spread from the individual to the affair that has been corrupted.

#### Subchannel E. Oath, Covenant, and Submission
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A binding word establishes obligation, and the obligated party yields or fulfills it.
- Active motifs: oath `ء ل ي:B007/m01`; covenant `ء م ه:B005/m01`; covenant or delivery `ق ض ي:B002/m01`; submissive hand `ي د ي:B006/m01`; bowing gesture of submission `ك ف ر:B014/m01`; submission to truth `م و ت:B013/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `ء ل ي` 80:24 `إِلَىٰ`; `ء م ه` 80:35 `أُمِّهِ`; `ق ض ي` 80:23 `يَقْضِ`; `ي د ي` 80:15 `أَيْدِي`; `ك ف ر` 80:17,42 `أَكْفَرَهُ/كَفَرَةٌ`; `م و ت` 80:21 `أَمَاتَهُ`.

### 10. Time, Threshold, and Crisis
- Semantic invariant: Events are located in durations and thresholds that can mature into appointed occasions, crises, completions, and aftermaths.
- Surface relation: direct; 80:22-23 marks deferred completion, 80:33-42 centers the catastrophic day, and 80:38-40 divides its facial outcomes.
- Surprising reach: Dawn is a face or unveiling, the day becomes an event rather than a duration, and withering or residue marks temporal completion.

#### Subchannel A. Dawn, Daybreak, and First Appearance
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Darkness opens and the first visible face of the day emerges.
- Active motifs: dawn `ف ج ر:B002/m01`; dawn brightness `س ف ر:B002/m01`; first signs of dawn `ب ش ر:B007/m01`; face or beginning of day `و ج ه:B007/m01`; daytime `ي و م:B001/m01`; shining emergence `ض ح ك:B006/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `ف ج ر` 80:42 `فَجَرَةٌ`; `س ف ر` 80:15,38 `سَفَرَةٍ/مُسْفِرَةٌ`; `ب ش ر` 80:39 `مُسْتَبْشِرَةٌ`; `و ج ه` 80:38,40 `وُجُوهٌ`; `ي و م` 80:34 `يَوْمَ`; `ض ح ك` 80:39 `ضَاحِكَةٌ`.
- Synthesis: Daybreak is simultaneously fissure, unveiling, brightness, and the forward face through which a new interval becomes visible.

#### Subchannel B. Duration, Delay, and Appointed Occasion
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: An action is held within a span, deferred, or released when its suitable time arrives.
- Active motifs: period of time `ء م م:B008/m01`; duration `ي و م:B002/m01`; appointed or opportune time `ء ب ب:B008/m01`; delay or detention `ء ل ي:B013/m01`; late urgency `ر ه ق:B004/m01`; waiting and deferment `ن ظ ر:B002/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `ء م م` 80:35 `أُمِّهِ`; `ي و م` 80:34 `يَوْمَ`; `ء ب ب` 80:31 `أَبًّا`; `ء ل ي` 80:24 `إِلَىٰ`; `ر ه ق` 80:41 `تَرْهَقُهَا`; `ن ظ ر` 80:24 `يَنظُرِ`.
- Synthesis: Time can contain action, postpone it, or intensify it as an appointed moment approaches and waiting collapses into urgency.

#### Subchannel C. Affair, Circumstance, and Event
- Reading type: surface-primary
- Scene or process: A matter gathers participants and conditions into an event that demands attention.
- Active motifs: affair or circumstance `ء م ر:B001/m01`; affair or concern `ش ء ن:B001/m01`; momentous event `ي و م:B003/m01`; historical elapsed time `غ ب ر:B002/m01`; fate-like event that destroys `ن ظ ر:B005/m01`.

- Synthesis: Duration becomes event when circumstances cohere into a consequential affair; afterward it enters history as elapsed time.

#### Subchannel D. Calamity, Dread, and Overwhelming Pressure

- Active motifs: calamity `ص خ خ:B004/m01`; deafening cry `ص خ خ:B001/m01`; grim day `ع ب س:B002/m01`; lasting dark calamity `غ ب ر:B005/m01`; monstrous affair `ء م ر:B006/m01`; burdening pressure `ر ه ق:B003/m01`; earth tremor `ء ر ض:B008/m01`.

### 11. Countenance, Surface, and Disclosure

### 12. Force, Contest, and Bodily Harm
- Semantic invariant: Concentrated force changes relations or bodies through strength, domination, weapon use, penetration, and injury.

- Active motifs: loud speech or noise `ب ر ر:B004/m01`; raised voice `ر ف ع:B010/m01`; deafening cry `ص خ خ:B001/m01`; silenced voice `ص د ي:B003/m01`; echo `ص د ي:B004/m01`; clapping `ص د ي:B008/m01`; animal teeth or sound `ض ح ك:B011/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `ب ر ر` 80:16 `بَرَرَةٍ`; `ر ف ع` 80:14 `مَرْفُوعَةٍ`; `ص خ خ` 80:33 `الصَّاخَّةُ`; `ص د ي` 80:6 `تَصَدَّىٰ`; `ض ح ك` 80:39 `ضَاحِكَةٌ`.
- Synthesis: The acoustic scene spans production, amplification, suppression, reflection, and rhythmic response, with animal noise extending the same sound mechanics.

#### Subchannel D. Rhetoric, Debate, Forgery, and Error
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Speakers construct, contest, or corrupt a verbal account.
- Active motifs: mutual debate `ن ظ ر:B010/m01`; fabricated speech `خ ل ق:B007/m01`; improvised speech `ق ض ب:B007/m01`; scribal or reading error `ص ح ف:B005/m01`; exclamation or lament `ش ي ء:B007/m01`; speech from the uvular region `ش ق ق:B008/m02`; witty or backbiting talk `ف ك ه:B007/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `ن ظ ر` 80:24 `يَنظُرِ`; `خ ل ق` 80:18-19 `خَلَقَهُ`; `ق ض ب` 80:28 `قَضْبًا`; `ص ح ف` 80:13 `صُحُفٍ`; `ش ي ء` 80:12,18,22 `شَاءَ/شَىْءٍ`; `ش ق ق` 80:26 `شَقَقْنَا`; `ف ك ه` 80:31 `فَاكِهَةً`.
- Synthesis: Discourse can be jointly tested in debate, generated on the spot, falsified, misread, or sharpened into exclamation and malicious wit.

#### Subchannel E. Song, Wit, Prosody, and Grammar
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Language and sound are patterned for aesthetic or formal effect.
- Active motifs: singing `غ ن ي:B003/m01`; witty entertainment `ف ك ه:B003/m01`; poetic meter `ق ض ب:B010/m01`; rhyme orientation `و ج ه:B011/m01`; grammatical nominative `ر ف ع:B012/m01`; delight or wonder `ض ح ك:B003/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `غ ن ي` 80:5,37 `اسْتَغْنَىٰ/يُغْنِيهِ`; `ف ك ه` 80:31 `فَاكِهَةً`; `ق ض ب` 80:28 `قَضْبًا`; `و ج ه` 80:38,40 `وُجُوهٌ`; `ر ف ع` 80:14 `مَرْفُوعَةٍ`; `ض ح ك` 80:39 `ضَاحِكَةٌ`.
- Synthesis: Song and wit supply performance, while meter, rhyme position, and grammatical elevation impose formal structure on the utterance.

### 15. Embodied Anatomy and Physiology
- Semantic invariant: Perception, speech, movement, reproduction, and injury depend on differentiated bodily parts and fluids.
- Surface relation: indirect; surface references to the blind person, human creation, seminal fluid, face, and hand activate a much wider anatomical network.
- Surprising reach: The network includes pupil, tear duct, uvula, esophagus, brain, skull suture, hip socket, chest, veins, and intimate anatomy.

#### Subchannel A. Eye, Pupil, and Tear Apparatus
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: The eye receives sight through a central pupil and is bordered by ducts or obstructing tissue.
- Active motifs: pupil `ء ن س:B005/m01`; pupil `ح د ق:B002/m01`; black center of the eye `ن ظ ر:B008/m01`; web over the eye `س ب ل:B010/m01`; tear ducts `ش ء ن:B005/m02`; bodily blindness `ع م ي:B001/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `ء ن س` 80:17,24 `إِنسَانُ`; `ح د ق` 80:30 `حَدَائِقَ`; `ن ظ ر` 80:24 `يَنظُرِ`; `س ب ل` 80:20 `السَّبِيلَ`; `ش ء ن` 80:37 `شَأْنٌ`; `ع م ي` 80:2 `أَعْمَىٰ`.
- Synthesis: Pupil and surrounding eye structure create the optical apparatus, while the web and blindness identify physical failure points.

#### Subchannel B. Teeth, Mouth, Uvula, and Esophagus
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: The mouth displays teeth, forms sound, receives food, and passes it through the throat.
- Active motifs: aligned white teeth `ح ب ب:B009/m01`; visible teeth `ض ح ك:B004/m01`; baring teeth `ف ر ر:B002/m01`; uvula `ش ق ق:B008/m01`; uvula `ل ه و:B004/m01`; esophagus `م ر ء:B005/m01`; seizing the throat `ط ع م:B012/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `ح ب ب` 80:27 `حَبًّا`; `ض ح ك` 80:39 `ضَاحِكَةٌ`; `ف ر ر` 80:34 `يَفِرُّ`; `ش ق ق` 80:26 `شَقَقْنَا`; `ل ه و` 80:10 `تَلَهَّىٰ`; `م ر ء` 80:34,37 `الْمَرْءُ/امْرِئٍ`; `ط ع م` 80:24 `طَعَامِهِ`.
- Synthesis: Teeth occupy the visible entrance, the uvula participates in sound at the throat, and the esophagus continues the passage of food into the body.

#### Subchannel C. Brain, Skull, and Hearing Center
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: The head encloses vulnerable cognitive and auditory tissue beneath joined cranial plates.
- Active motifs: brain `ء م م:B003/m01`; brain or hearing center `ص د ي:B002/m01`; skull sutures `ش ء ن:B005/m01`; wound approaching the brain `ن ط ف:B005/m01`; pointed horn or comb `د ر ي:B004/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `ء م م` 80:35 `أُمِّهِ`; `ص د ي` 80:6 `تَصَدَّىٰ`; `ش ء ن` 80:37 `شَأْنٌ`; `ن ط ف` 80:19 `نُطْفَةٍ`; `د ر ي` 80:3 `يُدْرِيكَ`.
- Synthesis: Sutured skull encloses brain and hearing center, while penetrating wounds and pointed implements define the vulnerability of that enclosure.

#### Subchannel D. Torso, Joint, Limb, and Hand
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: The body is stabilized by structural supports and acts through articulated limbs and skilled hands.
- Active motifs: ribs or bodily pillars `ب ن ي:B009/m01`; chest or thorax `ك ل ل:B007/m01`; hip socket `ك ر م:B007/m01`; hand as limb `ي د ي:B001/m01`; skilled hand `ي د ي:B015/m01`; handle or projecting hand of an object `ي د ي:B013/m01`; palm lines or thigh marks `ي س ر:B008/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `ب ن ي` 80:36 `بَنِيهِ`; `ك ل ل` 80:37 `كُلِّ`; `ك ر م` 80:13,16 `مُكَرَّمَةٍ/كِرَامٍ`; `ي د ي` 80:15 `أَيْدِي`; `ي س ر` 80:20 `يَسَّرَهُ`.
- Synthesis: Rib, chest, joint, limb, and hand form a support-and-action system, extended functionally to the graspable handles of tools.

#### Subchannel E. Generative Fluid, Intimate Anatomy, and Veins
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Bodily fluids and reproductive structures sustain generation and circulation.
- Active motifs: seminal fluid `ن ط ف:B002/m03`; semen as water `م و ه:B004/m01`; clitoris `ع ن ب:B005/m01`; phallus `ق ض ب:B009/m01`; male sex `ذ ك ر:B001/m01`; veins and erection `ن ش ر:B007/m01`; dripping sweat `ن ط ف:B003/m02`.
- Ayah anchors: `ن ط ف` 80:19 `نُطْفَةٍ`; `م و ه` 80:25 `مَاءً`; `ع ن ب` 80:28 `عِنَبًا`; `ق ض ب` 80:28 `قَضْبًا`; `ذ ك ر` 80:4,11,12 `يَذَّكَّرُ/تَذْكِرَةٌ/ذَكَرَ`; `ن ش ر` 80:22 `أَنْشَرَهُ`.
- Synthesis: Generative fluid, sexual anatomy, vascular expansion, and bodily dripping form a physiology of internal liquid moving toward reproduction or the skin.

### 16. Pleasure, Diversion, and Withdrawal
- Semantic invariant: Desire allocates attention toward enjoyment or away from obligation, producing delight, play, neglect, or abandonment.
- Surface relation: direct; 80:10 names distraction, while 80:31-32 names fruit and enjoyment and 80:39 depicts delighted faces.
- Surprising reach: Pleasure reaches witty talk, sexuality, luxury, mockery, respite, and the complete abandonment of a concern.

#### Subchannel A. Delight, Luxury, and Enjoyment
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A desirable object is enjoyed as pleasure, comfort, or refined living.
- Active motifs: delight and luxury `ف ك ه:B001/m01`; play, pleasure, or sexual enjoyment `ل ه و:B002/m01`; pleasure `ل ه ي:B002/m01`; utility and enjoyment `م ت ع:B001/m01`; softness and luxurious living `ن ع م:B002/m01`; delight or wonder `ش ي ء:B004/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `ف ك ه` 80:31 `فَاكِهَةً`; `ل ه و` 80:10 `تَلَهَّىٰ`; `م ت ع` 80:32 `مَتَاعًا`; `ن ع م` 80:32 `أَنْعَامِكُمْ`; `ش ي ء` 80:12,18,22 `شَاءَ/شَىْءٍ`.
- Synthesis: Fruit, comfort, play, sexuality, and luxurious softness occupy the positive side of desire as experienced enjoyment.

#### Subchannel B. Diversion, Neglect, and Abandonment
- Reading type: surface-primary
- Scene or process: Attraction to one concern displaces another until it is ignored or left behind.
- Active motifs: distraction `ل ه و:B001/m01`; heedless diversion `ل ه ي:B001/m01`; abandonment `ل ه ي:B003/m01`; deliberate neglect `ش ء ن:B006/m01`; turning away `و ل ي:B007/m01`; respite or postponement `م ت ع:B007/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `ل ه و` 80:10 `تَلَهَّىٰ`; `ش ء ن` 80:37 `شَأْنٌ`; `و ل ي` 80:1 `تَوَلَّىٰ`; `م ت ع` 80:32 `مَتَاعًا`.
- Synthesis: Diversion begins as displaced attention, becomes active neglect, and culminates in withdrawal, postponement, or abandonment.

#### Subchannel C. Wit, Laughter, and Mockery
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Speech produces amusement that may remain convivial or turn against a target.
- Active motifs: witty talk `ف ك ه:B003/m01`; backbiting `ف ك ه:B007/m01`; laughter `ض ح ك:B001/m01`; making another a laughingstock `ض ح ك:B002/m01`; delight and wonder `ض ح ك:B003/m01`; insolence `ف ك ه:B006/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `ف ك ه` 80:31 `فَاكِهَةً`; `ض ح ك` 80:39 `ضَاحِكَةٌ`.
- Synthesis: Wit and laughter form a shared entertainment scene whose social polarity changes when wonder gives way to ridicule, backbiting, or insolence.

### 17. Naming, Group Identity, and Place
- Semantic invariant: Names stabilize identity by attaching persons and groups to descent or locations to bounded terrain.
- Surface relation: indirect; the surah's kin terms and named human collectives provide surface anchors for latent clan names, place names, and settlement identities.
- Surprising reach: Plant, bird, terrain, and personal lexemes become labels for tribes, valleys, and settlements.

#### Subchannel A. Tribal and Clan Names
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A descent group is fixed and recognized through a conventional name.
- Active motifs: tribal name `ع ب س:B005/m01`; clan name `ن ب ت:B004/m01`; clan affiliation `ف ر ر:B014/m01`; lineage `ب ن ي:B007/m01`; community `ء م م:B004/m01`; collateral kin `ك ل ل:B004/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `ع ب س` 80:1 `عَبَسَ`; `ن ب ت` 80:27 `أَنْبَتْنَا`; `ف ر ر` 80:34 `يَفِرُّ`; `ب ن ي` 80:36 `بَنِيهِ`; `ء م م` 80:35 `أُمِّهِ`; `ك ل ل` 80:37 `كُلِّ`.
- Synthesis: Descent and collateral relation supply the social body, while the tribal or clan name gives that body a stable public identity.

#### Subchannel B. Named Places, Valleys, and Settlements
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A terrain feature or inhabited site acquires a fixed place-name.
- Active motifs: named place `ن خ ل:B006/m01`; place-name `ي س ر:B010/m01`; named valley or location `ق ض ب:B011/m01`; hill or place `ع ن ب:B004/m01`; remote village `ك ف ر:B012/m01`; residence `غ ن ي:B004/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `ن خ ل` 80:29 `نَخْلًا`; `ي س ر` 80:20 `يَسَّرَهُ`; `ق ض ب` 80:28 `قَضْبًا`; `ع ن ب` 80:28 `عِنَبًا`; `ك ف ر` 80:17,42 `أَكْفَرَهُ/كَفَرَةٌ`; `غ ن ي` 80:5,37 `اسْتَغْنَىٰ/يُغْنِيهِ`.
- Synthesis: Hill, valley, village, and residence become spatial identities when conventional naming fixes otherwise descriptive terrain.

### 18. Tracking, Riding, and Avian Life
- Semantic invariant: Animal movement becomes legible through pursuit, equipment, gait, calls, and species-specific form.
- Surface relation: indirect; 80:32 names livestock, while roots elsewhere in the surah open latent scenes of hunting, riding, and bird life.
- Surprising reach: The animal network assembles hunting blinds, prey verification, fitted saddles, owl calls, larks, ostriches, and small birds.

#### Subchannel A. Stalking, Hunting, and Prey Verification
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A hunter conceals approach, uses a blind and implement, strikes prey, and verifies death.
- Active motifs: concealing oneself to stalk prey `د ر ي:B003/m01`; hunting blind `ق ت ر:B002/m01`; hunting implement `ط ع م:B006/m01`; small arrows `ق ت ر:B006/m01`; exposing prey to death `ق ت ل:B005/m01`; verifying that prey has died `م و ت:B014/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `د ر ي` 80:3 `يُدْرِيكَ`; `ق ت ر` 80:41 `قَتَرَةٌ`; `ط ع م` 80:24 `طَعَامِهِ`; `ق ت ل` 80:17 `قُتِلَ`; `م و ت` 80:21 `أَمَاتَهُ`.
- Synthesis: Concealment and the hunting blind enable approach, the implement and arrow produce the strike, and inspection of the prey completes the hunt.

#### Subchannel B. Mounted Pursuit and Fitted Tack
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A horse is equipped for controlled, rapid travel or competitive pursuit.
- Active motifs: horse muzzle and gallop `ط ع م:B009/m01`; light responsive gait `ي س ر:B005/m01`; fitted saddle `ق ت ر:B008/m01`; saddle underlay `و ل ي:B011/m01`; accelerated travel `ر ف ع:B003/m01`; racing and overcoming `س ع ي:B008/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `ط ع م` 80:24 `طَعَامِهِ`; `ي س ر` 80:20 `يَسَّرَهُ`; `ق ت ر` 80:41 `قَتَرَةٌ`; `و ل ي` 80:1 `تَوَلَّىٰ`; `ر ف ع` 80:14 `مَرْفُوعَةٍ`; `س ع ي` 80:8 `يَسْعَىٰ`.
- Synthesis: Muzzle, saddle, and underlay fit rider to mount, while responsive gait and accelerated racing supply the movement outcome.

#### Subchannel C. Owls, Larks, Ostriches, and Bird Calls
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Distinct birds occupy a shared avian scene differentiated by size, species, and call.
- Active motifs: male owl `ص د ي:B001/m01`; lark `ق ب ر:B003/m01`; small bird `ف ر ر:B011/m01`; ostrich `ن ع م:B006/m01`; bird call `ش ق ق:B008/m03`; animal cry `ض ح ك:B011/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `ص د ي` 80:6 `تَصَدَّىٰ`; `ق ب ر` 80:21 `أَقْبَرَهُ`; `ف ر ر` 80:34 `يَفِرُّ`; `ن ع م` 80:32 `أَنْعَامِكُمْ`; `ش ق ق` 80:26 `شَقَقْنَا`; `ض ح ك` 80:39 `ضَاحِكَةٌ`.
- Synthesis: Owl, lark, small bird, and ostrich form a concrete species inventory, while calls and cries make their presence acoustically detectable.

### 19. Terrain, Wayfinding, and Habitation
- Semantic invariant: Uneven land is traversed by signs and paths, then bounded and built into places of residence.
- Surface relation: indirect; 80:20 supplies the path, 80:26 the earth, and 80:30 the enclosed gardens from which the latent terrain and habitation system expands.
- Surprising reach: Low ground, trackless steppe, sandy gaps, mountain passes, road signs, villages, leather shelters, and structural pillars form one spatial progression.

#### Subchannel A. Lowland, Slope, Gap, and Trackless Ground
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A traveler confronts terrain differentiated by elevation, opening, and navigability.
- Active motifs: low ground `ء ر ض:B001/m01`; slope or declivity `ص ب ب:B002/m01`; sandy gap with plants `ش ق ق:B007/m01`; trackless land `ع م ي:B006/m01`; hill or rise `ع ن ب:B004/m01`; valley or wadi `ق ض ب:B011/m01`; basin depression `ج ي ء:B002/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `ء ر ض` 80:26 `أَرْضَ`; `ص ب ب` 80:25 `صَبَبْنَا`; `ش ق ق` 80:26 `شَقَقْنَا`; `ع م ي` 80:2 `أَعْمَىٰ`; `ع ن ب` 80:28 `عِنَبًا`; `ق ض ب` 80:28 `قَضْبًا`; `ج ي ء` 80:2,8,33 `جَاءَهُ/جَاءَتِ`.
- Synthesis: Basin, lowland, slope, hill, valley, sandy opening, and trackless expanse define the changing physical constraints of traversable ground.

#### Subchannel B. Road Sign, Clear Course, and Mountain Pass
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A traveler selects direction by reading a marked route through obstructing terrain.
- Active motifs: road marker or sign `ء م ر:B005/m01`; clear road `ض ح ك:B005/m01`; mountain pass or wall `ك ف ر:B013/m01`; direction and course `و ج ه:B002/m01`; road `س ب ل:B001/m01`; far distance `ش ق ق:B005/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `ء م ر` 80:23 `أَمَرَهُ`; `ض ح ك` 80:39 `ضَاحِكَةٌ`; `ك ف ر` 80:17,42 `أَكْفَرَهُ/كَفَرَةٌ`; `و ج ه` 80:38,40 `وُجُوهٌ`; `س ب ل` 80:20 `السَّبِيلَ`; `ش ق ق` 80:26 `شَقَقْنَا`.
- Synthesis: Sign, direction, and clear course orient the traveler, while distance, wall, and mountain pass define the obstacles the route must negotiate.

#### Subchannel C. Settlement, Residence, and Agreeable Place
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Movement ends in a bounded place that supports dwelling and social continuity.
- Active motifs: remote village or settlement `ك ف ر:B012/m01`; residence `غ ن ي:B004/m01`; agreeable place of stay `ن ع م:B011/m01`; sacred building or Meccan locus `ب ن ي:B003/m01`; remaining fixed to place `ء ر ض:B006/m01`; neighboring dwellings `ن ظ ر:B003/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `ك ف ر` 80:17,42 `أَكْفَرَهُ/كَفَرَةٌ`; `غ ن ي` 80:5,37 `اسْتَغْنَىٰ/يُغْنِيهِ`; `ن ع م` 80:32 `أَنْعَامِكُمْ`; `ب ن ي` 80:36 `بَنِيهِ`; `ء ر ض` 80:26 `أَرْضَ`; `ن ظ ر` 80:24 `يَنظُرِ`.
- Synthesis: Fixed residence, neighboring dwellings, village, and sacred locus convert traversed terrain into a socially inhabited place.

#### Subchannel D. Building, Pillar, Shelter, and Enclosure
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Materials are raised and bounded to support, cover, and protect an inhabited interior.
- Active motifs: building `ب ن ي:B001/m01`; pillars or structural supports `ب ن ي:B009/m01`; leather shelter `ب ن ي:B004/m01`; surrounding enclosure `ح د ق:B001/m01`; protective screen `ك ل ل:B006/m01`; excavated well or hollow `خ ل ق:B011/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `ب ن ي` 80:36 `بَنِيهِ`; `ح د ق` 80:30 `حَدَائِقَ`; `ك ل ل` 80:37 `كُلِّ`; `خ ل ق` 80:18-19 `خَلَقَهُ`.
- Synthesis: Supports establish the frame, covering and screen protect it, enclosure marks its perimeter, and the well supplies a functional interior cavity.

## Standalone Subchannels

### S1. Diminutive Forms and Offshoots
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Smallness recurs across bodies, young animals, plant shoots, paths, projectiles, and quantities.
- Active motifs: insignificant or small entity `ء م م:B013/m01`; compact short body `ح ب ب:B010/m01`; little quantity `ي س ر:B002/m01`; young lamb `ء م ر:B009/m01`; minor branch roads `ب ن ي:B008/m01`; small palm shoot `ش ي ء:B006/m01`; small bird `ف ر ر:B011/m01`; small arrows `ق ت ر:B006/m01`; small snake `ق ت ر:B009/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `ء م م` 80:35 `أُمِّهِ`; `ح ب ب` 80:27 `حَبًّا`; `ي س ر` 80:20 `يَسَّرَهُ`; `ء م ر` 80:23 `أَمَرَهُ`; `ب ن ي` 80:36 `بَنِيهِ`; `ش ي ء` 80:12,18,22 `شَاءَ/شَىْءٍ`; `ف ر ر` 80:34 `يَفِرُّ`; `ق ت ر` 80:41 `قَتَرَةٌ`.
- Synthesis: Diminution is not one object class but one scale relation, linking offspring, offshoot, side-path, projectile, creature, body shape, and small amount.

### S2. Lots and the Apportioned Carcass
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Participants cast marked arrows for stakes and divide a slaughtered animal according to the resulting lots.
- Active motifs: gambling by arrows and division of a camel carcass `ي س ر:B007/m01`; small arrows `ق ت ر:B006/m01`; measured portions `ق د ر:B006/m01`; cutting into shares `ق ض ب:B001/m01`; livestock wealth `ن ع م:B005/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `ي س ر` 80:20 `يَسَّرَهُ`; `ق ت ر` 80:41 `قَتَرَةٌ`; `ق د ر` 80:19 `قَدَّرَهُ`; `ق ض ب` 80:28 `قَضْبًا`; `ن ع م` 80:32 `أَنْعَامِكُمْ`.
- Synthesis: Arrow, animal, cut, and measured share form a complete distributive game in which chance assigns portions of a common carcass.

### S3. Healing Incantation and Protective Treatment
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A vulnerable body is cleansed, anointed, or addressed by a curative formula after injury or pallor.
- Active motifs: healing incantation `ن ش ر:B008/m01`; purifying water or implement `ط ه ر:B004/m01`; oiling `ز ي ت:B003/m01`; medicinal coating or perfuming `خ ل ق:B010/m01`; pallor or harmful glance `ن ظ ر:B007/m01`; wound approaching the brain `ن ط ف:B005/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: `ن ش ر` 80:22 `أَنْشَرَهُ`; `ط ه ر` 80:14 `مُطَهَّرَةٍ`; `ز ي ت` 80:29 `زَيْتُونًا`; `خ ل ق` 80:18-19 `خَلَقَهُ`; `ن ظ ر` 80:24 `يَنظُرِ`; `ن ط ف` 80:19 `نُطْفَةٍ`.
- Synthesis: The treatment scene combines bodily vulnerability with cleansing, oil or coating, and a spoken curative act intended to restore the patient.
